# Consort Sends to FROM Royal Marriage
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.3
	title = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.3.t
	desc = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.3.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {	
	event_target:proposalcountry = {
		culture_group = japanese_g
	OR = {
		religion_group = christian
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_anathanism_active 
	}		
	}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OFFICIAL_JAPANESE_1_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {	
	event_target:proposalcountry = {
		culture_group = japanese_g
	NOT = {
		religion_group = christian
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_anathanism_active 
	}		
	}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OFFICIAL_JAPANESE_2_eventPicture
	}	
	goto = proposalcapital

	hidden = no
	
	trigger = {
	has_country_flag = cosmo_receive_proposal
	ai = no
	any_known_country = {
	has_opinion_modifier = {
		who = ROOT
		modifier = cosmo_sending_proposal
	}	
	NOT = {
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_marriage_proposal
	}
			OR = {
				culture_group = japanese_g

			}	
	}	
	}
	
		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_sending_proposal
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = proposalcountry
					capital_scope = {
						save_event_target_as = proposalcapital
					}
				}
						
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_receive_proposal		

			random_list = { #marriage

	title = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.301.t
	desc = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.301.d
	picture = {

	event_target:proposalcountry = {
		culture_group = japanese_g

		religion_group = christian

		ai = no

						continent = north_america
						}					
				}
				OR = {

					}
				}
				country_event = {

				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.23
					}
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_north_africa
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_europe
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_asia
				}				
				OR = {
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_namerica					
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_samerica					
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.24
				}
			}																									
	}		
}

			hidden_effect = {

					limit = {

								OR = {

								superregion = central_america_superregion
									}
							}
							}

					}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_province = {
								country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

								OR = {
								superregion = north_america_superregion
									}
							}
							}
							set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_north_america
						}																						
				}
				}

		name = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.24.a

					superregion = south_america_superregion

				}
				}

				}

		name = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.1001.a
		trigger = {
			has_consort = yes		
				}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_global_flag = cosmo_buildings_active
			}
			custom_tooltip = royal_visit_building_hmoonactive_tt
		}		
		event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = honeymoon_visit
				duration = 90 
				desc = honeymoon_visit_DESC
	
			}
		}										
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
					honeymoon_consort_bonus = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = zealot_personality  
						add_queen_personality = tolerant_personality 
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = babbling_buffoon_personality  
						remove_queen_personality = naive_personality  
						add_queen_personality = silver_tongue_personality
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = infertile_personality  
						add_queen_personality = fertile_personality
						}										
					}
					honeymoon_ruler_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
							change_dip = 1				
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
							change_adm = 1				
						}																		
						}
					honeymoon_province_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}	
			country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.1002
			    days = 30
				random = 15
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
			}					
}		
		option = {#end early
		name = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.1001.b
		remove_country_modifier = cosmo_on_honeymoon 
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				every_province = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
						}
					}
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop

						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {

						}
						}

							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler

			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.1003

				random = 15

						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}	
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				every_province = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
						}
					}
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
				}				
			}					
}			
}
